11:T51f,;(function () {
  try {
    var t = localStorage.getItem('theme')
    if (t === 'light' || t === 'dark') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add(t)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', t)
    } else if (t === 'system' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function') {
      var v = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light'
      document.documentElement.classList.add(v)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', v)
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark')
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem('sidebar-collapsed') === 'true') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-sidebar-collapsed', 'true')
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  // Reading mode: apply before paint so there is no flash even on full-page reloads.
  // The background inline style mirrors what ReadingModeContext sets via JS.
  // We read the CSS variable directly because the theme class is already on <html>.
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem('reading-mode') === 'true') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-reading-mode', 'true')
      document.documentElement.style.background = 'hsl(var(--background))'
    }
  } catch (e) {}

1c9:["$","blockquote","blockquote-1",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"通过使用各向异性过滤（Anisotropic Filtering），可以根据像素与纹理之间的角度来动态调整采样区域的形状，以更好地适应纹理的拉伸和变形。这意味着在沿着纹理的方向上，采样区域可以被拉伸，而在垂直于纹理的方向上则可以被压缩，从而提供更准确的采样。"}],"\n"]}]

1cc:["$","ul","ul-31",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":["Can look up ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"axis-aligned rectangular zones"}]]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":"Diagonal footprints still a problem"}],"\n"]}]
1cd:["$","blockquote","blockquote-2",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"className":"$undefined","children":"Ripmaps 和 Summed Area Tables（SATs）都是用于纹理映射的技术，旨在提高在不同分辨率下的纹理采样质量。"}],"\n",["$","ol","ol-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Ripmaps"}],":\n",["$","ul","ul-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":"Ripmaps 是一种用于在不同层次上存储纹理数据的结构。它将纹理分割成多个层次，每个层次的分辨率是前一个层次的一半。这种层次结构允许在不同分辨率下进行更有效的纹理采样。通过逐级插值，可以在不同分辨率的纹理之间平滑过渡，从而避免了突然的变化或失真。"}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Summed Area Tables (SATs)"}],":\n",["$","ul","ul-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":"Summed Area Tables 是一种用于快速计算图像区域内像素之和的数据结构。它通过在每个像素位置存储从左上角到该位置的所有像素之和来实现。这使得可以通过查询四个角的和并进行相减来快速计算出任意矩形区域内像素的总和。SATs 在纹理映射中通常用于快速计算矩形区域内的纹理印记，以便更有效地进行纹理采样。"}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]
1ce:["$","hr","hr-50",{}]
1cf:["$","p","p-234",{"className":"$undefined","children":["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"EWA filtering"}]}]
1d0:["$","blockquote","blockquote-3",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"EWA（Elliptical Weighted Average）滤波是一种用于纹理映射的高级采样技术，旨在提供更准确和更高质量的纹理采样结果。"}],"\n",["$","p","p-1",{"className":"$undefined","children":"EWA 滤波通过考虑采样点周围的纹理像素，并根据它们与采样点之间的距离和方向来加权平均这些像素的值。与传统的线性或双线性插值相比，EWA 滤波考虑了更多的信息，因此在处理各种纹理形状和方向时能够产生更好的结果。"}],"\n",["$","p","p-2",{"className":"$undefined","children":"主要特点包括："}],"\n",["$","ol","ol-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"椭圆权重"}],"：EWA 滤波使用椭圆形的加权函数来确定每个纹理像素对最终采样值的影响程度。这种权重考虑了采样点与纹理像素之间的距离和方向，使得在各向异性纹理和非均匀采样情况下能够产生更准确的结果。"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"自适应核大小"}],"：EWA 滤波通过自适应地调整椭圆核的大小和形状来适应不同的采样情况。这意味着它可以在不同分辨率和形状的纹理上产生更一致和更准确的采样结果。"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-2",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"抗锯齿和抗混叠"}],"：由于 EWA 滤波考虑了周围像素的权重和方向，因此它在处理锯齿状边缘和纹理混叠时能够产生更平滑和更真实的效果。"]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]
